# Project Tasks

Important: The AI assistant and any contributor must read this file first when starting work on the project. The AI should check task statuses here and continue the highest-priority task not marked DONE.

## Legend

- NEW — Task not started
- IN PROGRESS — Currently being worked on
- DONE — Completed
- REVERTED / FIXED / DEFERRED / ABANDONED — terminal states (per-entry note explains why)

## Phase note

**Phase 6 = Roadmap Cohesion** (2026-09-26, user decision): the `PETRIN_SVET_MASTER_EXECUTION_ROADMAP.md` is now the active product roadmap. The audit (`ROADMAP_AUDIT.md`) maps all 42 tasks to current code state. We work through Phase 1 (Product Cohesion) first, one task at a time: user approves → implement → user approves commit+sync → next task. Racing3D (Phase 5) is stable and awaiting user play-test feedback.

---

Never on errors; never continuous; never a replacement for semantic green/yellow. (Started 2026-10-09, DONE 2026-10-09 Ponytail Lazy Dev.) Single source `--ps-glow` in `accessibility.css` consumed by `.ps-focus-glow`, a shared six-state rule, and `:focus-visible` (its own `!important` rule, companions the yellow outline); per-page merges where the state rule already sets a box-shadow (sorting `.sort-selected`, spatial `.spatial-hint`, animals `.recog-hint`, classroom `.class-tab.active`, piano `.mode-btn.on`/`.song-chip.on` and `#modeFree.on` — the `#modeFree` id outranks `.mode-btn.on`, found because its screenshot stayed byte-identical) or in keyframes that animate box-shadow (shapes `hintPulse`, matching_game `hintGlow`). Guards: 4 new `hub_smoke` static checks (single source, six categories present, no error/success token or animation, cascade merges per page) + runtime computed-boxShadow checks in `sorting_smoke`/`memory_smoke`/`shapes_smoke`/`classroom_smoke`/`piano_smoke`/`rhythm_smoke`, all negative-tested (each fails alone on the mutated shape). Why: extend the V6.1 treatment from "defined" to "in use". Validation: `check_fast` 7/7; 15 affected smokes green; 12 review screenshots refreshed (rhythm/shapes/piano × 4 viewports, task 223 — classroom captures its hub, and coloring/animals capture states with no idle-visible glow).

- 222. DONE — Visual/UX plan **V6.1**: introduce the **Petrin Glow** — one reusable focus/attention treatment (spec §24). (Completed 2026-10-09, Ponytail Lazy Dev.)
    - `game/shared/accessibility.css` gains `.ps-focus-glow` with the spec's exact static contract (`box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgb(155 109 255 / 0.16), 0 8px 22px rgb(74 63 107 / 0.12)`). Static box-shadow (not animated) → no reduced-motion variant needed; deliberately **not** a semantic green/yellow, and applied transiently, never continuously (spec §24 requirements). Defined once in the shared base stack (`accessibility.css` is linked by all 24 child pages) so it is global with **zero** page edits.

Negative test: ring `3px`→`4px` + `spatial.html` link renamed → both checks red and correctly named (`glow contract missing`, `spatial.html`); reverted clean.

    - **CI red on the V2.5 push (`d1abefc`, run 37746441324) — fixed same day.** Release QA's Accessibility report failed with one serious `color-contrast` on `#coloringProgress`; every other page clean. **Root cause:** shell.css `.ps-status{opacity:0.7}` (V2.1) blended `--plum-soft` toward the cream background to ~3.1:1 — a latent shell flaw, because coloring is the FIRST page to render `.ps-status` (classroom has no status; puzzle predates `ps-*`), and the old `.coloring-progress` had no opacity (5.9:1, passed). Even `--plum` at 0.7 fails AA on cream, so opacity-based demotion cannot work here. **Fix:** removed the opacity from the shared `.ps-status` — hierarchy comes from size/weight/colour (status is smaller, 600 vs 800 weight, `--plum-soft` vs `--plum` title), never from opacity that erodes contrast. Verified: `node tools/axe_check.js coloring --report` clean (0 types, exit 0), `coloring_smoke` 28/28, `check_fast` 7/7 after `build_offline.js` + `sync-docs.sh`, coloring screenshots re-shot (the status is now fully opaque — a visible change). **Lesson: a change to a colour, font-size, or opacity in a shared style must run `axe_check` for the consuming page locally — `check_fast` does not cover contrast.** Fix pushed as `ffca55c` (+`138f628` docs); CI run `37747960047` green (Release QA + full matrix), CodeQL `37747960048` green, Pages `37747959812` deployed.

    - **V1.1 root cause confirmed with line numbers.** `game/games/animal_puzzle.js:340` initialises `rows`/`columns` to `GRIDS[0]`, which is the **object** `{rows:1, cols:2}`. `setGrid()` (line 344) is the only thing that converts them to numbers — but the initial-load path at line **598** calls `updateLabels(scenes[0])` with **no preceding `setGrid()`**, so line 353 renders `'Слагалица ' + (level+1) + ' · ' + rows + '×' + columns` as `Слагалица 1 · [object Object]×[object Object]`.
    - **V1.1 fix (two lines):** initialise to `GRIDS[0].rows` / `GRIDS[0].cols`, and call `setGrid()` before `updateLabels(scenes[0])` on the initial-load path.
    - **Why the existing smoke missed it — a test blind spot worth recording.** `puzzle_smoke.js` already asserted the exact label string, but it read the label *after* clicking `#sceneButton`, whose handler `startPuzzle()` calls `setGrid()` → `updateLabels()`. By then the values were already numbers, so the defect — which exists only in the window between page load and that first click — was invisible. **A check that reads state after an interaction cannot see a bug that the interaction itself fixes.**
    - **V1.1 new check reads the label in that window** (before any interaction): `puzzle_smoke` 22 → **23**. Proved non-vacuous by reverting the fix — the check went red with the exact string `Слагалица 1 · [object Object]×[object Object]`. Restored: **23/23**.
    - **V1.2 header rule.** The spec (§19) requires the title to own the activity name and the status to show scene position without grid internals. Changed `updateLabels()`: title is now always `Слагалица`, status is `1/8` (was `Слагалица 1 · 1×2` — the `1×2` is implementation-level grid internals the spec forbids exposing). Added a `@media(max-width:600px)` block so the header stacks title above status on phone portrait, preventing back-button collision.
    - **Smoke updated for V1.2:** the three label assertions now expect `1/8`, `2/8`, `8/8` and title `Слагалица`; the scene-cycling check now verifies grid sizes `[4,9,9,9,9,9,9]` (the cycle starts at level 1 because puzzle 1 was already completed earlier in the smoke) instead of scene titles.
    - **Validation:** `puzzle_smoke` **23/23**; `node --check` clean on both files.
- 215. DONE — CI: run only the smoke jobs affected by each push/PR instead of always running the full battery. (Completed 2026-10-07, Ponytail Lazy Dev.)

- 214. DONE — CI: parent_smoke.js was the only red leg out of 22 on the 2026-10-07 run (run 37604933349, push 367a984): `CDP Page.enable (id 3) got no response within 30000ms`, **0 checks**, exit 1, retried twice. Root cause: the three renderer-session enable commands (`Page.enable`/`Runtime.enable`/`Network.enable`) ran AFTER the `BOOT_ATTEMPTS` retry loop in `tools/headless.js`, so a Chrome whose browser process answered `Target.*` but whose renderer never enabled threw a bare 30 s timeout out of `start()` with zero assertions — and every `run_all.js` retry re-created Chrome only to hit the same wall. (Completed 2026-10-07, Ponytail Lazy Dev.)
    - **Fix:** the enables now sit INSIDE the boot loop's try block (plus the derived `c` handle), so a wedged renderer is a failed boot attempt: Chrome is killed, the next attempt starts fresh (headless.js:500–504, destructure at 527).
    - **Guard:** `tools/guards/boot_enable_negtest.js` — asserts the loop exists, the three `send(...)` calls sit before the `if (!session)` throw, the session object carries `c`, and by reverting to the pre-fix shape through a real edit (restored afterwards) proves the check actually detects the regression. Line-ending aware (CRLF/LF) and comment-safe (matches `send('Page.enable')`, not the word). Documented in `tools/README.md`.
    - **New standing rule (user request, part of this task):** before starting any new task, check git/CI status (`git status --short`, `gh run list`); if anything is failing or the tree is dirty in a way you did not create, report it and propose fixing it first — never build on a red pipeline or a stale tree. Added to AGENTS.md (Working rhythm) and CONTRIBUTING.md (Task lifecycle).
    - **Validation:** `node tools/guards/boot_enable_negtest.js` 5/5 PASS; `node tools/run_all.js parent_smoke.js` 31/31 (6.8 s); full battery `node tools/run_all.js` 34/34 tools, 835 checks, 212 s; `node tools/check_fast.js` 7/7 green. Local headless.js is byte-restored to the committed shape (guard writes it back after mutation).

- 212. DONE — Racing 3D: steering still wrong — the car "drif egz" the rear tosses out instead of turning to a side. Multiple prior iterations failed; rework the steer/turn model this round. (Completed 2026-10-07, Ponytail Lazy Dev.)
    - **Root cause (measured, not guessed):** the front wheels steered the WRONG WAY. With `kart.lookAt` the kart's local `+X` is its LEFT (`kart.lookAt` + forwarded-scratch measurement: `right = cross(tan,UP)`, local `+X` maps to world `−right`), so a positive `rotation.y` swings the wheel's nose toward the kart's left — ArrowRight pushed wheels LEFT while the body stayed pinned to the road tangent (`kartLean.rotation.y = 0`) and the kart slid right. That crossed-wheel crab IS the "drifts, rear tosses out" the user kept seeing. The smoke only asserted wheel *magnitude* (`stA.yaw > 0.1` = `steerYawValue`), never direction — a check that cannot fail.
    - **Fix:** front wheels now steer INTO the turn (`g.rotation.y = −steerYawValue`), and the BODY yaws about the REAR AXLE (task 212): new `kartYaw` (origin `z=−1.45`) + `kartBody` (counter-offset `z=+1.45`; all 14 `kartLean.add(...)` children re-parented to `kartBody`, zero coordinate changes, yaw=0 renders identically). Body yaw `kartYaw.rotation.y = −(steerDrive/STEER_YAW_MAX)·BODY_YAW_MAX` (0.22 rad bank untouched; bank test in scratch confirmed it already leans INTO the turn). Derived straight from eased `steerDrive` — no new state var, so `resetInput`/`resetSteerState`/frame-timing test all unchanged.
    - **Smoke upgraded to assert direction:** `steerState()` now returns `yaw` = real body yaw (was always-0 `kartLean.rotation.y`, making the old bank-release assertion vacuous) + new `wheelYaw` = applied front-wheel yaw. New steering asserts: `wheelYaw < −0.1 && byaw < −0.02` on ArrowRight, mirrored `> +0.1 / +0.02` on ArrowLeft, both decay < 0.05 on release. Verified: stA wheelYaw −0.43 / body −0.17, stLeft +0.43 / +0.17, bankB yaw 4.6e-7, boost flames still at anchors (flame=18), `racing3d_smoke` 34/34, `check_fast` 7/7 after `build_offline.js` + `sync-docs.sh`. Playtest screenshot at `.playwright-mcp/race-steer-right-212.png` (needs MiMo for visual review).

Build on `tools/screenshot.js` (which only captures `hub` + `screenshot:true` apps into `tools/screenshots/current/`) — extend or add a capture mode that opens EVERY page (all 24 child pages; add the hub and parent area), in 4 viewport/orientation combos (small + large × portrait + landscape), and saves the PNGs to a dedicated review folder. **User decision (2026-10-07, supersedes the 2026-10-06 "repo-root `review-screenshots/`" note): the review folder lives under `resources/General_reviews/` — a subfolder with a `TABLE_OF_CONTENT.md` (details per screenshot: game, page, viewport/orientation) plus all PNGs — committed and pushed like normal code, not published to Pages, not git-ignored. The process must be repeatable and documented (same tool for future UI reviews), running on the user's host via Live Server / headless.js.** Additionally (2026-10-07): if any screen is later updated, that task must also refresh that screen's screenshots and commit them; new screens must be recorded; the TOC must carry the **date and time of the last screenshot** and the **task that did the change** as columns. This rule is now part of the implementation definition of done (see AGENTS.md "Review screenshots are part of a change's definition of done"). **Folder name decision (2026-10-07, user): the review folder keeps a STABLE generic name (`Screenshot_Review`), not a date, because the data inside changes — a later task refreshes this same folder (via `--review=Screenshot_Review --pages=… --task=…`) instead of making a new one each time.**

- 209. DONE — Classroom (mališane) activities: the items sitting in grid containers are crowded / overlapping / clipped by the container edges on small screens. Fix the container grid so it is comfortable on phones. (Completed 2026-10-06, Ponytail Lazy Dev.)
    - **User report:** kids-fit items in the classroom business grids crowd / overlap / clip, especially phones.
    - **Root cause:** `game/shared/accessibility.css` lines 2–7 apply a global `button { min-width:64px; min-height:64px; padding:10px }`, which forces `.class-tile` / `.kids-option` wider than their grid tracks on narrow screens (measured 64px tiles in ~40px tracks at 360–640). The same flex rule let `#kidsPrompt` (button) shrink its content, so a 20-emoji count row overflowed the card; and `#classroomHub` centered with `margin:auto` in a scroll container started at a clipped negative top on tablet portrait (768×1024 hub was 1354px tall, top −165).
    - **Fix (all in `game/pages/classroom.html`):** (1) `.activity-grid .class-tile, #kidsOptions .kids-option { min-width:0; min-height:0; padding:0; }` — tile tracks now rule; (2) `#kidsPrompt { flex-shrink:0; }`; (3) `.kids-count-row` is now `display:grid; repeat(10, minmax(0,1fr)); justify-items:center; gap:0.6vmin 0.8vmin; font-size:5vmin; max-width:72vmin` — plain `1fr` has a min-content floor (~26px per emoji) that over-constrained the grid; `minmax(0,1fr)` + 5vmin font caps the emoji advance (21.97px) inside the track pitch (23.3px) with the row at most as wide as the prompt; (4) `#game-classroom { justify-content:flex-start; }` (+ `#classroomHub { margin:auto }`) — the auto-margin is ignored by the scroll container, so the hub now top-anchors when overflowing instead of starting above the fold.
    - **Guard:** `classroom_smoke.js` gained 3 permanent geometry checks (task 209 block): a 360×640 phone session asserts (a) all 21 activity tiles are pairwise disjoint, inside the grid, and max tile width < 64px (regression: the global button floor), and (b) with the numbers pool forced to count ≥ 10, the emoji count row does not clip `#kidsPrompt` and max span-box overlap < 2px (regression: 6.3px crowding); a 768×1024 tablet session asserts the overflowing `#classroomHub` starts at top ≥ 0 (reachable) — regression: negative top.
    - **Validation:** `node tools/classroom_smoke.js` — ALL PASS (29 checks). `node tools/check_fast.js` 7/7 green after `node tools/build_offline.js` + `bash tools/sync-docs.sh` (254 docs files). The throwaway geometry probes (`tools/probe_*.js`) were deleted after use.

- 208. DONE — Spatial «Простор»: five concepts, now each with THREE scenes — 15 rounds before anything repeats. (Completed 2026-10-06, Ponytail Lazy Dev.)
    - **User report:** "the compass game (Простор) is too short; add more content/rounds."
    - **Root cause:** each of the five concepts had exactly ONE scene, and `round = (round + 1) % 5` cycled them; a session repeated the same five pictures forever.
    - **What shipped:** `MODES[i].rounds[]` — every concept keeps its original R26 scene as `rounds[0]` (fresh boot identical) and gains two new scenes with a different object, position-pair and Cyrillic prompt: updown ⚽/🍎 на дрвету/🦆 на огради; insideoutside ⚽ у корпи/🐰 у кутији/🐤 у гнезду; leftright ⚽/🦴/🧀; nearfar ⚽/🐤 паче/🧶 клупко; frontbehind 🐱 иза миша/🐰 испред куће/🦊 испред дрвета. Advance is now `(round + 1) % 15`, so normal play crosses variant boundaries (round 4 → 5 is the same concept, new scene). New decor CSS: `.sp-tree`, `.sp-fence`, `.sp-box`, `.sp-nest`, `.sp-house`. Every scene stays self-explanatory by position alone (no reading required).
    - **API:** `window.__spatial.state()` gained `variant` and `total` (15). `setRound` normalizes mod 15. The original fields are untouched, so the offline spec (tap the marked answer) needed no change.
    - **Smoke:** `spatial_smoke` walks all 15 rounds and asserts each concept appears exactly 3 times with at least 2 distinct prompts (real content, not copies) and that a correct answer advances past the first pass into a new scene — **45/45**.
    - **Validation:** `check_fast` 7/7 after `build_offline.js` + `sync-docs.sh`; offline E2E 140/140 (`spatial PASS played`).

- 206. DONE — Rhythm is now a real mini drum set: four deep-to-high pads, free-play + repeat-melody modes, melody grows 2→6. (Completed 2026-10-06, Ponytail Lazy Dev.)
    - **User report:** "the rhythm/drum game is not really good, instead of one drum maybe to have more than one like some deep sound and a higher sound like a real drum set, and with two options, free play and repeat melody where the melody to be repeated is slowly increasing in complexity."
    - **What shipped:** `rhythm.js` rewritten around a 4-pad drum set — Велики бубањ 🥁 (130 Hz) → Средњи (220) → Мали добош (340) → Високи звук 🔔 (540), rendered as graduated circular pads (32→23 vmin, warm→gold gradients) so bigger/left = deeper, like a kit. Two modes via a segmented `mode-bar`: **🎵 Понови мелодију** (echo: the app plays a random pad melody with never the same pad twice in a row, the child echoes it; a wrong pad gently replays the melody, never advances) and **🥁 Слободно свирање** (every pad just plays its sound). **Complexity grows slowly:** melody length starts at 2 and grows one pad per success, capped at 6. No clock, no score; celebrate every third success. Replay 🔁 plays the current melody.
    - **API ripple:** `window.__rhythm.state()` now returns `{mode, phase, round, length, pattern, step, successes}` with the live melody; `setMode`/`setRound` replace the old `setPairSet`/`tapDrum` and `setRound`'s old `%2` semantics. The old `#drum-area` single drum is gone.
    - **Smokes rebuilt to match:** `rhythm_smoke` asserts boot into a 2-pad echo melody, 4 pads + 2 mode buttons reachable (geometry `elementFromPoint`), Cyrillic prompt with no score/timer, a wrong pad re-listen-replays without advancing, three successful echoes (2→3→4 pads) with celebrate-on-third, the 6-pad cap via `setRound(9)`, free play sounding all four drums, switching back to echo, and the repeat button. The offline E2E rhythm spec now echoes the live `pattern` on `[data-idx]` pads instead of tapping `#drum-area`.
    - **Two real bugs found by the new smoke:** (a) first version set `phase='listen'` only at replay start, but for the 750 ms wrong-pad pause the phase still read `repeat`, so the following echo raced mid-replay — the smoke now waits `listen`→`repeat` explicitly; (b) `setRound(n)` normalized via `n % 6`, so `setRound(9)` landed at length 5, unreachable cap for the test — now `Math.max(0, n)` with the `Math.min(2+round, 6)` cap intact.
    - **Validation:** `rhythm_smoke` 21/21; `node --check` clean; offline E2E via run_all 140/140 (rhythm `PASS played`, exit 0, 24.3 s); `check_fast` 7/7 after `build_offline.js` + `sync-docs.sh`.
    - **Follow-up a11y fix (2026-10-06):** the new echo-mode `#echo-btn` (`.mode-btn.active`) used `background:var(--green); color:#fff` — white on `#67C971` is ~2.06:1, failing WCAG AA and reddening CI's `Release QA` a11y report (`axe_check.js --report`, serious color-contrast, `#echo-btn`). The `--green` win-modal buttons in explorer/classroom pass only because those modals start `display:none` (invisible to axe); this pill is visible on load. Fixed locally in `rhythm.html`: active mode bar uses `#2E7D32` with white text (~5.1:1). `node tools/axe_check.js --report` → all 25 pages clean, no serious/critical violations.
- 205. NEW — Sequencing: once an item is dragged to its correct square it disappears; it should stay visible inside the correct square. Acceptance: assertion in the smoke that a placed card remains a visible child of its slot.

- 205. DONE — Sequencing: a correctly placed card now stays visible inside its square. (Completed 2026-10-06, Ponytail Lazy Dev.)
    - **User report:** "постави у редослед, once I drag an item to correct square it disappears, it should be visible in the correct square". Confirmed as a real rendering bug, not an invariant failure: the `placed` counter was correct, only the DOM made the card invisible.
    - **Root cause:** `tryDrop` absolute-positioned the card at `(slot.left - tray.left, slot.top - tray.top)` and relied on the tray as the containing block, but `.seq-tray` has **no `position:relative`**, so the positioned card resolved against the fixed `#app`. The slots sit *above* the tray, so `top` went **negative** and the card rendered off the top of the viewport inside `overflow:hidden` — invisible. Task 200's sorting game never had this bug because it appends the placed item into the basket slot (`sorting.js:236`); sequencing predates that pattern.
    - **Fix:** on a correct drop move the card into the slot itself (`slot.el.appendChild(card.el)`) and hide the slot's number span — the same `appendChild` pattern as sorting — instead of pixel-positioning it. All drag/placement logic is unchanged.
    - **The smoke could not have caught it:** it asserted only the `placed` counter, which was correct all along. New per-drop assertions verify the card is (a) a child of its slot, (b) geometrically inside the slot's rect, and (c) the topmost hit at the card's centre (`elementFromPoint`) — so an "invisible drop" regression stays red.
    - **Validation:** `sequencing_smoke` **21/21** (3 new visibility checks); `node --check` clean on `sequencing.js` and `sequencing_smoke.js`; `docs/` synced (254 files).

- 197. DONE (part A + part B) — Tier A item 2: numbers 11–20 recognition. (2026-10-05, Ponytail Lazy Dev.)
    - **Part A DONE — the stale assertion is fixed.** `tools/classroom_smoke.js:62` pinned `SW.tiles === 11`, a hardcoded copy of today's vocabulary length: adding 11–20 would have turned it red for a change it was never meant to police. It now reads `SERBIAN.numbers.length`, and a **new** check asserts the numbers activity renders **every shared number, in order** — the invariant a bare count cannot see.
    - **Both new assertions proved non-vacuous.** With the reverse-order mutation injected into `classroom.js` (committed and unmodified beforehand, so `git checkout` restored it exactly), the smoke went red with `rendered ["10","9",…,"0"] expected ["0","1",…,"10"]` — **while the count check still passed**, which is precisely the gap the new check closes: a classroom rendering the right *number* of the *wrong* numbers. Restored, `classroom_smoke` **20/20**.
    - **Part B BLOCKED — and it is a hard blocker, not a judgement call.** Adding 11–20 to `SERBIAN.numbers` makes `shared/speech.js`'s `registerEach` **throw at script load**, because it enforces `items.length === files.length`. Measured: `numberFiles` and `sentenceFiles` in `speech.js:33-36` hold **11 entries each**, and `game/assets/audio/speech/` contains recordings for **0–10 only** (`jedan.mp3` … `deset.mp3` and the 11 sentence clips; nothing for 11–20). **19 of 24 pages load `shared/speech.js`**, so the throw would break nearly the whole app, not just the Classroom — a far worse regression than the stale assertion this task started from.
    - **Unblocked 2026-10-06 — the blocker was resolved, and the search that "proved" it was blind.** The history search for TTS tooling missed `resources/tts_generate.js`: a Google Translate TTS generator (`client=tw-ob`, `tl=sr`) that ships in the very first commit `d02c6e2` and wrote the MP3s to `game/assets/audio/speech/`. It was missed because it **never mentions any of the searched engines** (`espeak`/`festival`/`piper`/`gtts`/`flite`/`coqui`/`macos`) — it fetches Google's endpoint directly and parses the MPEG frame sync. A live probe proved the endpoint still answers from this host. `classroom_smoke.js` line 62 had already been de-hardcoded (part A), so the fix was the pure data change the roadmap predicted: **add 32 pairs to the generator's `pairs` list, run it (32 new MP3s, 0 failed), extend `serbian.js` `numbers` to 0–20 and `speech.js` `numberFiles`/`sentenceFiles` to 21 each.** Now the numbers activity shows 11–20 and the entire kids quiz pool grew to 20 countable items. The earlier "cannot" conclusion was wrong in a specific, useful way: the *specific* claim "no TTS binary exists on this host" was true (espeak/ffmpeg absent), but the *general* claim "no TTS tooling has ever existed" stopped at the wrong result set.
    - **20 numbers (11–20) were generated as 10 name + 10 sentence clips** (`jedanaest`, `dvanaest`, `trinaest`, `cetrnaest`, `petnaest`, `sesnaest`, `sedamnaest`, `osamnaest`, `devetnaest`, `dvadeset` + the `_pasa`/`_macaka`/… sentence forms), plus Time (`jutro/dan/vece/noc`) and Seasons+weather (`prolece/leto/jesen/zima` + `kisa/sneg/sunce/vetar`) in the same pass — task 198's whole recording backlog, done locally. `registerEach` verified 21==21 and threw on nothing.
    - **Validated end-to-end.** `classroom_smoke` (21 tiles + every number in order + kids numbers quiz / countable emoji row), `tracing_smoke` (numbers counter de-hardcoded to `SERBIAN.numbers.length` — it pinned `1 од 11`), `counting_smoke` (10-cap untouched, still green), `check_fast` 7/7 (after `build_offline.js` + `sync-docs.sh`). `animal_counting`'s `maxLevels = 10` cap is deliberately untouched until 11–20 recognition is established in the classroom.
    - **False alarm resolved during this investigation (recorded so it is not repeated).** A `git log --diff-filter=D` sweep surfaced `be.mp3`, `ce.mp3`, `dje.mp3`, `dze.mp3` and 3 more as *deleted* files and it looked like 7 letters had no recording. They do not: `speech.js` maps `'b'` → `b.mp3` (short name), and the `-e` suffixed variants were historical files removed long ago. A programmatic check of all 6 registered asset lists confirms **0 missing** across 30 letters, 30 words, 11 numbers, 11 sentences, 10 shapes and 11 colours (109 MP3s on disk). Lesson: a filename appearing in a *deletion* diff is not the same as an asset a module *requires* — verify the mapping, not the git history.

Verified all four positional lists: **shapes 10/10** correct (`Круг→krug` … `Пирамида→piramida`), **colours 11/11** correct (`Црвена→crvena` … `Црна→crna`), **numbers 11/11** and **sentences 11/11** in order, and the alphabet's 30 Cyrillic→Latin sound mappings (`Ђ→dj`, `Љ→lj`, `Њ→nj`, `Ћ→cj`, `Џ→dz`, `Ж→zh`, `Х→h`, `Ц→c`) all correct. `animals` maps by id rather than index, so it cannot drift that way. **No drift found.**

That value was correct only *coincidentally* — 9 live games routes plus 1 entry in `RETIRED_ROUTES`. Adding one more games-category game, precisely what R21 did for the learning count, would have made the readiness wait never satisfy and turned a **correct hub into a red smoke**. Now derived from `GAMES_EXPECTED.length`.
      - `coloring_smoke.js` pinned `U.palette === 11`, while `coloring.js:27` builds the palette from `SERBIAN.colors` — the same frozen-vocabulary-size mistake. Now derived from `SERBIAN.colors.length`.
    - **Both proved non-vacuous together.** Re-injecting the stale literals (`=== 11` for hub games, `=== 12` for the palette) reproduced both traps exactly: the hub readiness wait reported `ready:false` with `active:"hub-games"`, and the palette check reported `rendered 11, SERBIAN.colors has 11`. Restored the derived versions: `hub_smoke` **15/15**, `coloring_smoke` **23/23**, `check_fast` **7/7**.

- 200. DONE — Fix touch dragging in sorting + sequencing (user-reported). (Completed 2026-10-05, Ponytail Lazy Dev.)
    - **User report:** the sorting game's drag (red→red, animals→animals) does not work. Confirmed as a real product bug, not a flake.
    - **Root cause:** both draggables left `touch-action` unset. The drag is implemented with pointer events, and when a draggable element does not declare `touch-action:none`, a **touchscreen** browser claims the gesture for panning/zooming and fires `pointercancel` part way through — so the item stops following the child's thumb and the drop never lands. With a mouse this never happens, which is why every smoke stayed green.
    - **Why no test caught it:** `tools/headless.js` drives real **mouse** input only — it has no touch emulation (its only `Emulation` call is `setDeviceMetricsOverride` with `mobile:false`). A finger-only defect is invisible to the entire battery by construction.
    - **Fix:** `touch-action:none` plus explicit `user-select:none` on `.sort-item` (`game/pages/sorting.html`) and `.seq-card` (`game/pages/sequencing.html`). Sequencing had the identical latent gap and was fixed in the same pass.
    - **New checks assert the COMPUTED style**, which is the only way to pin a CSS-level touch contract without a touch-emulating runner: `sorting_smoke` asserts `getComputedStyle('.sort-item').touchAction === 'none'` and `user-select === 'none'`; `sequencing_smoke` asserts the same for `.seq-card`.
    - **Proved non-vacuous:** with `touch-action` reverted on both pages, the checks went red with the exact offending values — `.sort-item` computed `manipulation`, `.seq-card` computed `auto`. Restored, `sorting_smoke` **21/21** and `sequencing_smoke` **15/15**.
    - **Correction recorded rather than repeated:** I initially assumed `touch-action` is not an inherited property and wrote that into a comment. Measurement contradicted it — `.sort-item` computed `manipulation` (from the page's `html,body` rule) while `.seq-card` computed `auto`. Only the verified facts are kept in the smoke comments, and the assertion is on the value (`none`) rather than on a theory about why it was wrong.
    - **Ruled out while investigating:** the user's other report about a highlight that persists after the image changes is **not** sorting — `sorting.js:281` calls `setSelected(null)` in `newRound()`, and `setSelected` removes the class from the previously selected element, so the selection ring is cleared correctly.
    - **Still open, needs user clarification:** (a) the request to present the options "under a mathematical question like 4x6=__", and (b) "the presenting window sometimes does not show all on smaller devices", and (c) which game has the stale yellow highlight around a chosen answer. None of these could be attributed to a specific page with confidence, and guessing would risk changing the wrong game.
- 201. DONE — Parent gate, parent-page small-screen clipping, compare's stale yellow ring, smaller candy hint. (Completed 2026-10-05, Ponytail Lazy Dev.) — all four user-reported.
    - **Parent gate (user request 1).** A one-question speed bump in front of the parenting actions, so a small child cannot reach «Преузми за офлајн рад» or «Обриши локални напредак». **Deliberately multiplication**, per the user's reasoning that a child who already knows addition and subtraction could walk straight through a sum. Six facts (`6×7 … 9×6`), three plausible near-misses (`answer+a`, `answer-b`, `answer+10`), and the correct option's position shuffled every render so it cannot be learned. **Not a lock:** no timer, no attempt limit, no penalty — a wrong answer shakes and re-asks (`Покушај поново`).
    - **The actions are `hidden`, not `disabled`** — there is nothing focusable or tappable for a stray tap or a screen reader before the question is answered. `#parentGate` and `#parentActions` are mutually exclusive.
    - **Deliberately silent:** no speech is triggered, because new Serbian words cannot be recorded in this repo (task 198). The gate must not depend on an asset that does not exist.
    - **Small-screen clipping (user request 2) — same page, same root cause.** `html,body` was `overflow:hidden` and `#app` centred its flex child, so on a short viewport the bottom of the card was unreachable **and the top was clipped off-screen** with no scrollbar to reveal either end. Fixed by making `#app` scroll (`overflow-y:auto`) and moving centring from `justify-content:center` to `.parent-card{margin:auto}` — the centred-flex overflow trap, where an over-tall child loses its TOP and stays unreachable even inside a scroll container. The back button moved `absolute` → `fixed` so it does not scroll away, plus a `@media (max-height:620px)` block tightens the card. Verified at **390×480**: `overflowY:auto`, `cardTop:16` (not negative), last action reachable after scrolling.
    - **Compare's stale yellow rectangle (user request 3) — root cause was NOT a game class.** `shared/accessibility.css:10` paints `outline: 4px solid #FFD23F` (that is the yellow) on `button:focus`, and `.cmp-group` **is** a `<button>`. Tapping gave it focus; `clearMarks()` removed the `cmp-wrong/cmp-hint/cmp-correct` classes but never blurred, so the outline survived `newRound()` and stayed wrapped around a group already refilled with new objects — reading as "that one is still selected". Fixed by blurring the groups/answer buttons in `clearMarks()`. **Keyboard focus visibility is deliberately untouched:** focus is only dropped at the moment the content is replaced, where retaining it on a stale target is wrong anyway.
    - **Candy hint button 30% smaller** (user request 4): `7.6vmin` → `5.3vmin`, font `4.4vmin` → `3.1vmin`, shadow scaled to match. Every dimension moved together so the glyph and its hit area stay in proportion — shrinking only the font would have left the tap target *looking* large while not being so.
    - **New checks, all proved non-vacuous by reverting the fix:**
      - `parent_smoke` (21 → **31**): 6 gate checks (actions unreachable before answering, asserted with `offsetParent === null` **and** `elementFromPoint`, not mere presence; multiplication not addition; wrong answer does not unlock; correct answer reveals and makes them tappable; answer position varies) + 3 small-viewport checks in a **second 390×480 session**. Reverting the gate's unlock turned the reveal check red; reverting to `overflow:hidden` + centred flex turned 2 viewport checks red with `cardTop:-66`, reproducing the reported clipping exactly.
      - `compare_smoke` (25 → **27**): asserts a group *can* hold focus, then that no group still holds focus once the round advances. Reverting the blur reproduced the report exactly: `activeIsGroup:true, outlined:true`.
    - **A test blind spot worth remembering:** every pre-existing `parent_smoke` check uses `.click()`, which bypasses hit-testing — so all 21 passed unchanged with the actions behind a `hidden` gate. A gate that is not present at all would also have passed. The new checks assert reachable state specifically because presence-based ones could not have noticed.
    - **Validation:** `parent_smoke` 31/31, `compare_smoke` 27/27, `sorting_smoke` 21/21, `sequencing_smoke` 15/15, `candy_smoke` 13/13, `check_fast` **7/7**, `validate_generated` 4/4, `docs/` synced, inventories 221 / 220.
- 202. DONE — Tier B item 5: more sequencing content, and repair the offline gate the parent gate broke. (Completed 2026-10-05, Ponytail Lazy Dev.)
    - **Two more sequences in `game/games/sequencing.js`** (`fruit`: Цвет → Мали плод → Зрела јабука; `birds`: Јаје → Пиле → Птица), taking it from 2 to 4. This is Tier B item 5 and it needed **no new speech**, because a step's `label` is used only as an `aria-label` (verified at the card markup) while the game speaks just the fixed prompt plus the already-recorded praise/retry lines. **Kept every sequence at 3 steps deliberately** — the layout is sized in `vmin` for three, and this project has already been bitten twice by small-viewport clipping.
    - **Caught my own duplicate accessible names before shipping:** the first draft of `fruit` used `Јабука` for both step 1 and step 2, which would have given two cards the same accessible name. Now `Мали плод` / `Зрела јабука`.
    - **Two more stale-literal traps found and fixed, both caused by adding content:**
      - `offline_smoke.mjs` pinned the sequencing step count with `.seq-slot').length===3`. Now asserts the real invariant — slots and cards are equal and non-zero — so any future step count works.
      - `sequencing_smoke.js` waited for `round===0` after completing the second sequence, which was only ever true **because there were exactly two**. With four, the second completion advances to round 2, so the check failed while the game was perfect. It now asserts the actual invariant: *the round advances to a different value and `placed` resets to 0*.
    - **New check covers every sequence, not the one on screen** (`sequencing_smoke` 15 → **16**): all four build with 3 cards / 3 slots, `order` values exactly `0,1,2` with no duplicates (a duplicate order means two cards are "correct" and one slot can never be filled), and unique `aria-label`s.
    - **Regression I caused and repaired:** the parent gate from task 201 put the offline-download and update-check controls behind a question, which broke `offline_smoke`'s parent flow in **three** places. The subtle one: the tool navigates to the parent page **twice** (once online to prime the cache, once offline to prove the controls survive) and the gate is per page load, so answering it once is not enough — the second visit reported «Провери ажурирања» as `element has zero size` while the first visit's gate had already been passed. Fixed with one `unlockParentGate(h, label)` helper called at both sites, which answers the question the way a parent would (reading it off the page) rather than deleting the gate or forcing a button visible — so the offline gate still proves the controls are genuinely reachable once unlocked.
    - **Validation:** `sequencing_smoke` **16/16**; `offline_smoke` **134 pass / 3 fail**, all three the established `racing3d`/WebGL host limit and nothing else; `runtime_error_smoke` **29 pass / 2 fail**, likewise racing3d only; `check_fast` **7/7**; `validate_generated` 4/4; `docs/` synced; inventories 221 / 220.
    - **Host note:** during this task `runtime_error_smoke` swung 27/4 → 18/13 → 29/2 across runs on identical code, which is the load-flakiness signature. It settled back to the exact 29/2 baseline once the host recovered, and `pgrep` found no orphan Chromium — so it is environmental, consistent with the three clean-worktree comparisons already made this session.

      - `speech.js:25-28` — pronunciation for **all 30** letters (`letterFiles`) and all 30 example words (`wordFiles`) is already registered to real Serbian MP3s, and `registerEach` **throws** if the two lengths ever diverge.
    - **`SERBIAN.alphabet` is complete and correctly ordered:** all 30 letters `АБВГДЂЕЖЗИЈКЛЉМНЊОПРСТЋУФХЦЧЏШ`, each with an example word that starts with it — **0 violations**, 30 distinct first letters, every entry has an emoji. It was already the correct single source of truth.
    - **BUG FOUND AND FIXED — 6 of the 10 shipped phonics entries taught a false association.** `А→Јабука` (starts Ј), `С→Змија` (З), `Т→Ауто` (А), `К→Мачка` (М), `Р→Зец` (З), `И→Сова` (С). In a toddler letter-sound game that is the worst kind of wrong: it teaches a link that is simply untrue in Serbian.
    - **Why it shipped: a claim with no check behind it.** `PROJECT_TASKS` task 189 recorded the set as "Correct item actually starts with the letter in question (10-letter set verified …)" and `phonics.js` carried a `Verified:` comment — but `phonics_smoke.js` only ever asserted `s.letter.length === 1 && s.sound.length > 0`. Nothing checked correct-start, so the verification existed only in prose. This is the project's own recurring failure mode (a check that cannot fail), reached through a comment rather than an assertion.
    - **Fix: derive instead of duplicate.** `LETTERS` is now `window.SERBIAN.alphabet.map(...)`, so every correct item is the alphabet's own example word — correct-start **by construction** — and phonics can no longer drift from classroom/tracing. Coverage went from 10 to all **30** letters with no new page, hub entry, registry change or smoke. The distractor rotates `+7` through the alphabet, so it is always a *different* letter's word; since every word begins with its own distinct letter, a distractor can never collide with the letter being asked about.
    - **Pronunciation now comes from the recorded assets.** `sound` is the entry's lowercase name (`а`, `б`, …), which `speech.js` has already mapped to a real MP3. Previously `'Ммм...'` matched no asset and silently fell through to `speechSynthesis`. This is the "correct pronunciation of a letter" the task required, taken from assets that were already in the repo.
    - **The check that should have existed in R23 now exists.** `phonics_smoke.js` walks **all 30** rounds and asserts: the letter equals `SERBIAN.alphabet[i]`, the correct item's name starts with it, the distractor does not, and `sound` equals the alphabet entry's name (i.e. is the registered recording). Also asserts `alphabet.length === 30`.
    - **De-hardcoded a stale literal in the same file:** the "correct advances" check computed `(sc.round + 1) % 10` — the cycle length was pinned to the old 10-letter set and would have asserted the wrong thing the moment it grew. It now reads `ALPHABET_LEN` from the alphabet.
    - **Proved the new check is non-vacuous.** With the fix in place `phonics_smoke` is **17/17**. Then the historical bug was re-injected (a correct item replaced with `Јабука`, which starts with Ј) and the smoke went **red**, naming the exact offender: `Б: correct item Јабука starts Ј`. The mutation was reverted and 17/17 restored.
    - **Validation:** `phonics_smoke` **17/17**; `classroom_smoke` 19/19; `tracing_smoke` 26/26; `check_fast` **7/7**; `docs/` synced; inventories 221 / 220.

The baseline is far worse than the changed tree, so the timeouts are host-load flakiness — this host degraded over the session (`hub_smoke` 472 s vs 6 s, runtime scan 362 s). No orphan Chromium was found. Worktree removed afterwards.

- 204. DONE — Fix Release QA's mid-session CDP crash + the CI deprecation warnings and runner-migration notice. (Completed 2026-10-06, Ponytail Lazy Dev.)
    - **The red job was Release QA's offline E2E dying with a raw crash, not a failed assertion.** `offline_smoke.mjs` printed 3 disk/registry PASS lines, then `Error: CDP websocket closed unexpectedly (ws://127.0.0.1:<port>/devtools/browser/…) — code=1006 reason=(none) wasClean=false preceded-by=(nothing logged) browser=Chrome exited code=0 signal=null`, `[Symbol(cdp-socket-gone)]: true`, `Node.js v22.23.3` — an uncaughtException report with **no `[headless] Unhandled CDP failure:` line anywhere**. Every one of the 27 smoke-matrix legs on the same host was green. The project had already marked this exact shape as the open root cause in `AGENTS.md`.
    - **Root cause closed.** `headless.js`'s task-177d absorber handles `unhandledRejection` — a promise left UN-awaited. But every `offline_smoke` interaction `await`s its in-flight command, so when the socket dies the rejected await **throws** at the tool's top level. For a `.mjs` tool top-level await is module evaluation, and Node reports the rejection as a fatal **uncaught exception** that the `unhandledRejection` absorber never sees. Added a matching `uncaughtException` net in `headless.js` that recognises the `CDP_FATAL` symbol, reports the failure once, and sets exit code 1 — anything else still crashes, so a real harness bug is never hidden. `guards/cdp_fail_negtest.js` still **7/7** after the change.
    - **`run_all.js` now retries a mid-session CDP death exactly like a boot crash.** The environmental test grew the last clause: `code !== 0 && ((pass === 0 && fail === 0) || /no assertions ran/ || (fail === 0 && (/CDP websocket (closed unexpectedly|error)/ || /Chrome exited while a CDP session was open/)))`. A tool that printed even one FAIL is never retried, and the retry budget is still 3 total attempts, so a *deterministic* regression still goes red — proved with a throwaway `_cdp_death_probe_smoke.js` (always-dies fake): run_all printed `[mid-session browser death … retrying (1/2) (2/2)]` and reported FAIL, never a false green. The probe was deleted; it ended with `_smoke.js` and started with `_`, so it was never part of the battery.
    - **CI hygiene (lets fix all).** `actions/checkout@v4` → `@v5` and `actions/setup-node@v4` → `@v5` (both Node 24 majors) — kills the **36 × "Node.js 20 is deprecated … forced to run on Node 24"** warnings. `runs-on: ubuntu-latest` → **`ubuntu-24.04`** in all five jobs — kills the **21 × "ubuntu-latest will migrate to Ubuntu 26 on 2026-10-19"** notices and pins CI to a known image. `tools/validate_workflow.js` — which asserts LF-only, no tabs, runs-on present, needs-direct-deps, matrix-from-battery — passes **11/11** after the edits (no rule was touched).
    - **Validation:** `cdp_fail_negtest` **7/7**; `validate_workflow` **11/11**; `offline_smoke` via `run_all` **140/140, exit 0 in 23.5 s**; `check_fast` **7/7**. The `headless.js`/`run_all.js` /`ci.yml` changes only — no `game/` content touched, so `docs/` and the offline inventories are untouched. Local run uses this host's CRLF worktree, so `validate_workflow`'s "no CR" still passes because `.gitattributes` pins `ci.yml` to LF in the checkout.
- 195. DONE — Clear the two red findings left by R26/R27. (Completed 2026-10-05, Ponytail Lazy Dev.)
    - **Fix 1 — `check_all.js --docs --offline` generated a red gate by construction.** It ran `sync-docs.sh` then `build_offline.js`, but `build_offline.js` rewrites the two inventories inside `game/` and explicitly "writes nothing to `docs/`" (its own header). So the documented pre-commit ritual left `docs/` stale by exactly those 2 files and the next `validate_generated` — which is what CI's `fast` job runs — failed with `2 differ, e.g. offline-manifest.json, sw-cache-list.json`. The two steps are now reordered (offline rebuild + inventory validation, *then* the mirror), with the reason recorded at the call site. Verified end-to-end by deleting both `sw-cache-list.json` files and running the real ritual: rebuild → validate → sync, `docs/ now mirrors game/ (222 files)`.
    - **Fix 2 — `guards/chrome_diagnostic_negtest.js` failed for the wrong reason.** Its end-anchor was the literal `'\n  }\n  if (!session)'`, but `tools/headless.js` is **CRLF** in this worktree, so `indexOf` returned `-1`, `decl < -1` was false, and the check reported `declared 76 chars into the loop` — implying the declaration was buried deep in the loop body, when it was in fact `headless.js:412`, the **first** statement inside the loop. The code was always correct; the gate was lying. Fixed at the single read point (`readFileSync(...).replace(/\r\n/g,'\n')`) so every later anchor is LF-normalised — the same CRLF class as the `.gitattributes` footgun, and a gate must not depend on the worktree's line endings.
    - **Proved the repaired guard is not vacuous, rather than assuming it.** Against the correct tree it is **10/10**. Then `tools/headless.js` was mutated to hoist `let exitEvent = null;` out of the boot loop (the pre-fix bug) and the guard was re-run: **8/10, with 2 failures** — the text check *and* the behavioural `the report never names the poisoned attempt-1 exit`. So it catches the real regression, not merely the source shape. `headless.js` was then restored and confirmed byte-identical to HEAD.
    - **Result:** `check_fast` **7/7**; all **5/5** guards green for the first time (`cdp_fail`, `chrome_diagnostic`, `games_map`, `registry_guards`, `route_contract`). Regeneration reproduced HEAD's inventories exactly (220 entries), confirming determinism and leaving no residue.
    - **Process note recorded for the next session:** never pipe a long-running node tool through `head` — closing the pipe raises SIGPIPE and kills the tool mid-run. An early `check_all` verification was silently truncated this way and left `docs/` half-synced; it was caught only because `git status` disagreed. Redirect to a file and read the file instead.
- 194. DONE — R27b: Reassess the original 9-game learning roadmap. (Completed 2026-10-05, Ponytail Lazy Dev.)

Linked from `ROADMAP.md`. Docs-only: no `game/` change, so no sync or inventory regen was needed and `check_fast` stays 7/7.

World is declined outright by §41 ("Avoid a quiz-heavy geography app for very young children").

The win: `classroom.js:41` builds the numbers activity generically over `SERBIAN.numbers`, so adding 11–20 to the data lights up 11–20 recognition **with zero game code**.
    - **Landmine recorded before it bites:** `tools/classroom_smoke.js:62` hardcodes `SW.tiles === 11`. Adding 11–20 makes that 21 and reds the assertion, so it must be de-hardcoded against `SERBIAN.numbers.length` first — the same lesson R21 applied to `hub_smoke`'s `LEARNING_EXPECTED`. A count that pins today's value is a check that cannot survive the change it is supposed to allow.
    - **Evidence of genuine absence, not assumed absence:** a case-insensitive search of `game/data/serbian.js` for `jutro|veče|noć|dan|jesen|zima|proleće|letnji` returns **zero** matches and `Object.keys(SERBIAN)` has no time- or season-shaped key, so Time and Seasons are absent at the data layer too — not merely unbuilt. `classroom.js:38-43` defines only `alphabet`, `numbers`, `shapes`, `colors`.
    - **Doc bug found:** the original roadmap labels the Seasons item **"Сечења"**, which in Serbian means *cutting* — a mistranslation of "Seasons". §41 uses the correct `Годишња доба`. Recorded so a future session does not search for the wrong concept.
    - **Recommendation:** Tier A — extend `phonics` to all 30 letters, then 11–20 number recognition (leaving `animal_counting`'s cap alone until recognition is established). Tier B — Time, Seasons, plant growth/habitats. Tier C — explicitly **do not build**: a standalone colors app, math equations (§41 forbids the ordering), continents/countries, and a word-building app (§41 warns it assumes literacy).
    - **Roadmap queue is now complete (R0–R27b).** No further items exist in the Fresh Elevation Roadmap; new work comes from Tier A/B above or from the user's own direction.
- 193. DONE — R27: Maze / Path Following Pilot. (Completed 2026-10-05, Ponytail Lazy Dev.)
    - **New learning game.** `game/pages/maze.html` + `game/games/maze.js` (`window.startMaze`). The child drags a bear 🐻 stone-to-stone along a wide path of rounded stones from a 🚩 start to a 🏠 house. Roadmap §40 asks for "a large, forgiving path. No timer. No deadly traps. A wrong path can gently redirect the child", and the shapes follow directly from that.
    - **No dead ends by construction.** Each maze is a list of direction runs on a grid (`[['right',3],['down',1],…]`), expanded into the ordered cells the bear must touch — so the path *is* the solution. There is nothing to get stuck behind and no failure state to reach.
    - **Genuinely forgiving.** The snap radius is `0.72` of a cell and is measured in **cell-normalised** units (`distanceInCells`), so "forgiving" means the same thing on a phone and a desktop instead of drifting with viewport width. The bear is clamped to the board while dragging, so it can never be dragged out of sight and lost. A 🔄 retry button is always available.
    - **Gentle redirect, not punishment.** A drop more than a snap away slides the bear back to the last reached stone (`gentleMiss` + `Покушај поново`), the round does not advance, and nothing is lost. After two misses `speakSr('retry')` is spoken. No failure screen, no score, no timer.
    - **Fine-motor input.** Real `pointerdown`/`pointermove`/`pointerup` with `setPointerCapture` (the same gesture model `sequencing.js` uses), `touch-action:none` on both the stage and the token so a drag never scrolls the page, and the position maths derives from the stage's own `getBoundingClientRect` so it is correct at any viewport.
    - **Wiring:** entry `maze` in `game/data/app-registry.js` (learning, `route: game-maze`, `hubOrder: 14`, `back: maze-back`, `start: startMaze`), a 🧩 button appended to the learning sub-hub in `game/index.html`, and `maze: 'Путања'` in `game/data/serbian.js`.
    - **Tests:** new `tools/maze_smoke.js` (**19 checks**) — boot, retry/back geometry via `elementFromPoint` **and** in-viewport, token size, no score/timer, Cyrillic prompt, stone count equals the cell count with the house on the last stone, the off-path redirect (bear returns to the last stone, `reached` unchanged, `misses` incremented), a full drag-to-the-goal solve, praise on arrival, retry reset, the celebration cadence, and that all three mazes build with their own ids. `offline_smoke.mjs` gains a `maze` spec that performs a **real trusted drag** along one stone using the game's own waypoint geometry (not hard-coded pixels) and now reports **23 registry children, 23 specs, 24 hub buttons**; `validate_pages.js` 24 pages; `check_fast.js` **7/7**.
    - **Three defects I introduced and fixed, recorded because the rule matters more than the diff.** (a) The smoke shadowed the harness's imported `start()` with a local `const start`, which threw `Cannot access 'start' before initialization` — and `run_all` correctly reported it as a 0-check boot crash rather than a real failure. (b) The stone assertion used `.maze-stone:last-child`, which matches **nothing** because the token is the stage's last child; the page expression threw and `h.evalv` handed back an object instead of a string. Stones are now indexed. (c) The same line used `'\\u{1F3E0}'` inside a template literal, which is a literal backslash sequence, not 🏠 — the assertion could never have matched the house it was meant to check.
    - **Validation:** `maze_smoke` 19/19; `check_fast` 7/7; offline E2E 132 pass with `maze` reporting `PASS played` (23 games); `runtime_error_smoke` 29 pass / 24 pages. `offline_smoke` and `runtime_error_smoke` remain red on this host **only** on `racing3d`/WebGL — the same 3 + 2 counts proven pre-existing twice (clean worktree at `e367f7a`, and again by R26), now 127→132 and 28→29 passing with zero new failures. `docs/` synced; inventories 221 / 220.
    - **Screenshot baseline deferred** (`screenshot: false`) — blocked on a WebGL-capable host; see the DECISION entry below.
    - **Acceptance met (roadmap §40):** a large forgiving path, no timer, no deadly traps, gentle redirect on a wrong path; teaches visual following, path planning and fine-motor control.
    - **Task number is 193, not the roadmap's "suggested 178"** — 178 was already spent on the CDP `describe()` diagnostic, the same drift the tracker table warns about.
- FINDING (2026-10-05) — **RESOLVED by task 195.** `check_all.js --docs --offline` used to regenerate the offline inventories in `game/` *after* mirroring `game/` into `docs/`, so following the documented pre-commit ritual guaranteed a red `validate_generated`. The two steps are now reordered. Keep the invariant: **`build_offline.js` before `sync-docs.sh`**.
- 192. DONE — R26: Spatial Concepts Pilot. (Completed 2026-10-05, Ponytail Lazy Dev.)
    - **New learning game.** `game/pages/spatial.html` + `game/games/spatial.js` (`window.startSpatial`). Five modes cycle — `горе/доле`, `унутра/ван`, `лево/десно`, `близу/далеко`, `испред/иза` — exactly the roadmap §39 list. Each round is a short scene plus two large Serbian-Cyrillic words to choose between, so the meaning is carried by the picture and the child names it, per "scenes and movement rather than text-heavy instructions".
    - **The scene cannot contradict the answer.** Each mode's `build(answerFirst)` returns the correct layout *and its mirror*, and the scene is drawn from the same `correct` value the buttons are labelled from — so a mode cannot describe a position it did not render. The scene is a fixed `aspect-ratio` stage, so percentage positions mean the same thing in every viewport.
    - **Gentle, as R21–R25.** Wrong tap shakes only the pressed button (`gentleMiss`, no failure screen, no scoring, round does not advance); after two misses the correct word is hinted (`showHint` + `speakSr('retry')`). Correct plays `successChime()` + `speakSr('praise')` and advances after a short pause; every third correct answer fires `window.celebrate()` (recorded by R19). Both buttons lock on success so a double tap cannot score two rounds — the same defect that once made one ➡️ tap advance three scenes. No score, no timer.
    - **Wiring:** entry `spatial` in `game/data/app-registry.js` (learning, `route: game-spatial`, `hubOrder: 13`, `back: spatial-back`, `start: startSpatial`), a 🧭 button appended to the learning sub-hub in `game/index.html`, and `spatial: 'Простор'` in `game/data/serbian.js`.
    - **Tests:** new `tools/spatial_smoke.js` (**41 checks**) — boot, choice geometry asserted with `elementFromPoint` *and* in-viewport (not presence-only; `headless.boxOf` scrolls, so a clipped control would otherwise pass while no child could tap it), the exact Serbian word pair for all five modes, exactly-one-correct, gentle miss with no advance, shake, hint-after-two, correct-locks-both-buttons, answer alternation, and the celebration cadence. `offline_smoke.mjs` gains a `spatial` spec and now reports **22 registry children, 22 specs, 23 hub buttons**; `validate_pages.js` 23 pages; `check_fast.js` **7/7**.
    - **Two stale assertions fixed, both mine, neither a product bug** (recorded because the fix is the rule, not the diff): (a) a first draft waited for "the third celebration", but `successes` is a session counter that deliberately survives `setRound` and the earlier checks had already spent some of it — the cadence is now asserted as a rule over four successive answers; (b) the first version of that rule compared the **cumulative** celebration count against a 0/1 flag, so it demanded the count fall back to 0 after the third success. Both were test bugs; the game was correct in each case.
    - **Regression evidence, not assumption.** `offline_smoke.mjs` and `runtime_error_smoke.js` each report red on this host, all of it `racing3d` (`THREE.WebGLRenderer: A WebGL context could not be created`). Verified pre-existing rather than assumed: a clean `git worktree` at `e367f7a` (pre-change) reproduces **exactly** the same failure counts (3 offline, 2 runtime). With the change: offline 122→127 pass, runtime 27→28 pass, **zero new failures**. `spatial` reports `PASS played` offline.
    - **Screenshot baseline remains deferred** (`screenshot: false`) — see the DECISION entry below: the refresh is blocked on a WebGL-capable host, not deferred by choice.
    - **Acceptance met (roadmap §39):** all five concept pairs taught in Serbian Cyrillic through scenes, no text-heavy instructions, no timer, no score.
- DECISION (2026-10-05, user) — the visual baseline refresh deferred by R21–R25 is **BLOCKED ON HOST CAPABILITY, not deferred by choice. Do not run `tools/visual_compare.js --approve-baseline` on this Alpine Linux host.**
    - Measured 2026-10-05 at commit `e367f7a`: a read-only `node tools/visual_compare.js` compares 85 images and **fails all 85**. Baselines were captured on the user's Windows host, so every page drifts 1–18% from font rasterisation and Chrome build alone.
    - `racing3d` is the dangerous one: 4 of 5 sizes change **97.8–99.0%** (~5x the worst other page), and the capture log shows `THREE.WebGLRenderer: A WebGL context could not be created`. This host has no WebGL, so approving here would replace the reviewed 3D renders with the «3D није доступан овде» fallback — a silent, permanent regression committed as the new *approved* reference.
    - Approving would also be unreviewed: baselines require human review, and image review needs a vision-capable model.
    - It would not even achieve the goal: the five new pilots are `screenshot: false`, so they are **not** in the 85-image set; a refresh only re-captures pages already opted in.
    - **Correct procedure:** flip `screenshot: true` for a page, then capture and approve from a WebGL-capable host (the user's Windows machine, or a GPU CI runner). Never capture baselines on a WebGL-less host.

- 183. DONE — R17: PWA Install / Update / Version QA. (Completed 2026-10-04, Ponytail Lazy Dev.)
    - **One source of truth, no hand-maintained version string.** `game/manifest.json` `"version": "2.0.0"` is the only place the app version is written. Pages register the service worker as `sw.js?v=<version>` (`shared/navigation.js` fetches `manifest.json` first, then registers with the query), and `sw.js` derives `CACHE_NAME = 'petrin-v' + SW_VERSION` from its own script URL (`self.location.href`). The name is computed at **evaluation on every wake-up**, not during `install`: a worker is re-evaluated per event and keeps no module state, so a name resolved once in `install` would be lost.
    - **Cache lifecycle proven under a repository subpath.** `activate()` claims clients first, then deletes every `petrin-*` cache that is not the current name, and (only when something was actually superseded, i.e. not a first install) posts `{type:'updated', version}` to all clients. `sw_update_smoke.js` serves the app at `/some/deep/prefix/` and asserts `scriptURL=.../sw.js?v=2.0.0`, cache `petrin-v2.0.0`, **exactly one** `petrin-*` cache, 207 entries, and the full diff lifecycle (0 → 1 changed → removed-file reported → 0 again). This empirically confirms Chromium preserves the `?v=` query on the worker script URL; without it the name would silently fall back to `petrin-v0`.
    - **Parent UX (Serbian, per project rule):** a new `offline-copy-info` meta item shows `Офлајн копија: спремна` / `није преузета` (detected from Cache Storage — presence of `offline-manifest.json`), `check-result` with `changed>0` now says `Постоји нова верзија` and relabels the button `⬆️ Ажурирај офлајн копију`, finishing the re-cache says `Ажурирано`, and a worker `updated` message also confirms `Ажурирано`. Technical facts (version, cache names, SW scope, online state) moved into a `<details class="diagnostics">` disclosure so they are available but not in a parent's face.
    - **Dead duplicate wiring removed (`shared/navigation.js`).** Its legacy offline block (`#download-offline`/`#download-status`) also ran on `parent.html`, which has no `#download-status`, so it attached a **second** `cacheAll` click handler to the same button — every download ran twice. It now bails with `if (!btn || !status) return;`; the parent page's own controls are the single owner.
    - **Tests updated, not loosened:** `parent_smoke.js` now asserts the offline-copy line, the diagnostics disclosure, and the update flow by dispatching the exact letters the worker posts (`check-result changed:2` → `Постоји нова верзија`; a relabelled button; `cache-complete` → `Ажурирано`; `updated` → `Ажурирано`, button back to `📥 Преузми за офлајн рад`). `offline_smoke.mjs` no longer hard-codes `petrin-v2` — it discovers the active `petrin-*` cache, so a version bump cannot silently invalidate the offline gate.
    - **Validation (2026-10-04):** `parent_smoke.js` 14/14 PASS; `sw_update_smoke.js` 11/11 (subpath); `offline_smoke.mjs` all 16 games + 103 checks green; `check_fast.js` **7/7** after `docs/` sync and offline-inventory regeneration. `node --check` clean on `sw.js`, `navigation.js`, `parent_smoke.js`, `sw_update_smoke.js`, `offline_smoke.mjs`.

Its build step shelled out to `zip`, which is not on PATH here, so `check_all.js --offline` could never complete on this host. It was also the sole cause of a locally-red `check_fast.js`: the stray `docs/game-offline.zip` failed `validate_generated.js` ("docs/ has no extra file not in game/"). **The user's instruction was to remove the option rather than make the gate green.**
