## History (closed tasks, ordered by number)

### 103. DONE — Testing tooling upgrade (2026-09-25, Ponytail Lazy Dev)

Dev-only testing tooling, plus **one real user-facing bug found and fixed** in the process. (Started 2026-09-24, user approved all 4 workstreams + the WebKit download + the axe-core CDN fetch via the question tool; finished 2026-09-25 after an interrupted session.)

**Delivered:**
- `tools/run_all.js` — parallel smoke runner. Every `tools/*_smoke.js` already booted its own unique-profile Chrome on its own debug port, so the battery is concurrency-safe. Capped worker pool (default 4), per-tool `PASS/FAIL` table, non-zero exit on failure. Flags: `--game <substr>`, `--since <sha>` (git-diff → static game-file→smoke map; `game/shared/*` and anything unmatched expand to the whole battery), `--watch` (mtime-poll `game/**`, re-runs only affected smokes), `--concurrency N`, `--list`, plus positional smoke names.
- `tools/check_all.js` — the one-command ritual: `node --check` over `game/**` + `tools/**` `.js`/`.mjs` → `run_all.js` → optional `--docs` (`tools/sync-docs.sh`) / `--offline` (`tools/build_offline.ps1`). Passes `--game`/`--concurrency` through.
- `tools/play_matrix.mjs` — Playwright device matrix: 5 viewports (phone portrait/landscape, tablet portrait/landscape, desktop) × engines (chromium via system Chrome + webkit) × pages, asserting no uncaught/console errors, no horizontal overflow, and touch points (chromium only — Playwright's WebKit desktop build always reports `maxTouchPoints === 0`). Resolves Playwright from the npx cache the MCP already populated, so the repo still has no `package.json` and no deps. WebKit installed (`npx playwright install webkit`).
- `tools/axe_check.js` — axe-core (WCAG 2.0/2.1 A+AA) scan over the existing headless harness. axe-core is fetched **once** from jsDelivr and cached in the git-ignored `tools/.cache/`. Informational by default, `--report` to gate.
- `.opencode/skills/validate-game-change/SKILL.md` — the validation ladder (smallest relevant subset → `--watch` → full `check_all.js`), how to read FAIL vs boot-crash, and the rule that stale assertions get fixed to observed truth, never the game bent to fit a test.
- `tools/headless.js` — boot hardening (`--no-default-browser-check`, `--disable-background-networking`, `--disable-component-update`, `--disable-default-apps`, `--disable-sync`, `--disable-features=Translate,MediaRouter,OptimizationGuideModelDownloading`), debug-port poll 8×250 ms → 20×100 ms, plus two additive exports: `serve()` (the static server, so Playwright/a11y tools reuse it) and `evalp()` (promise-awaiting eval, needed because `axe.run()` is async).
- `.gitignore` — `tools/.cache/`.

**Bugs found and fixed (not tooling — real defects surfaced by the new runner):**
1. **`game/shared/main.js` booted every standalone page up to 3×** (immediate `tryStart` + `DOMContentLoaded` + `load`, each calling `window.startX()` with no guard). `startColoring` and `startTracing` re-add their click/pointer listeners on each call, so **one real tap on ➡️ advanced 3 animals/shapes** (verified live: 1→4→7→10→12 per click). Fixed at the root with a `started` flag in `tryStart`, so all 14 standalone pages boot exactly once.
2. **`tools/racing3d_smoke.js` never exited** — it was the only smoke with no `h.close()` / `process.exit()`, so the process hung until the runner's 300 s cap killed it *after* printing 27/27 PASS. Added the teardown.
3. **7 stale static assertions** against `game/shared/main.js`'s 3-field `standaloneMap` (it gained a 3rd field on 2026-09-11): `animals_smoke.js:72`, `coloring_smoke.js:80`, `adventure_smoke.js:244`, `dino_smoke.js:338`, `driving_smoke.js:191`, `piano_smoke.js:134`, `shapes_smoke.js:87`. Also `tracing_smoke.js` hard-coded the hub's `data-go` count at 20 (now 22) — the assert now derives the count from `index.html` so it cannot go stale again.
4. **Boot-crash retry in `run_all.js`** — a smoke exiting non-zero with **0 checks** never got its assertions run; its Chrome failed to launch under parallel load. Those are now retried twice and only reported if they fail a third time (verified: a real boot crash recovered to 22/22 PASS).

**Validation:** `node --check` clean on every touched file; `node tools/run_all.js` = **19/19 tools PASS, 361 checks, 0 fail** (also closes the batch-10 pending reruns from task 102); `node tools/play_matrix.mjs` = **50/50 cells OK** (chromium + webkit, zero horizontal overflow, zero console errors); `node tools/axe_check.js` ran over 7 pages. NOT committed (user commits/pushes).

**Follow-up found by `axe_check.js`, NOT fixed (needs user decision — it touches every page):** every page except `animal_memory.html` has `<meta name="viewport" content="… maximum-scale=1, user-scalable=no …">`, i.e. **pinch-zoom is disabled site-wide** — a real WCAG 1.4.4 (Resize Text) *critical* violation, 1 node × 6 pages. `coloring.html` additionally has a *serious* `color-contrast` violation on `#coloringProgress`. Recorded as task 104 below.

### 21. DONE — Coloring game: fix blank first screen (2026-08-02, Copilot)

The redrawn `path` regions stored `d` flat on the region object while `createColoringRegion` only read `r.attrs`, so `Object.entries(undefined)` threw on the first Dog region and aborted the scene build — palette rendered but both SVGs stayed empty. Fix: `createColoringRegion` now uses `r.attrs || r` and skips `tag`/`color` keys. Verified headlessly in Chrome: first scene renders name + 10 reference regions + 10 play regions; all 11 scenes / 109 regions build clean. node --check passes.

  - **Procedural background music (2026-08-03, user request "simple whimsical game music inline with each level's theme"):** new offline Web Audio music engine in papper_kitty.html — no audio files, all synthesized. Each world got a `music:` key mapped to a theme in a `MUSIC` config: spring/summer/autumn/winter/underground/cave/water/savanna/japan/northpole/egypt. Each theme = short 2-bar loop (16 melody steps + 8 bass beats) with a per-world root, tempo (76–132bpm), scale and waveform: bright major pentatonic for Spring, Summer, Winter, North Pole, Water; natural-minor melancholic for Autumn; low sparse minor for Underground/Cave; warm A-pentatonic for Savanna; hirajoshi for Japan; phrygian-dominant/exotic for Egypt; sleigh-bell register for North Pole. `startMusic(theme)` called from `loadWorld()` swaps the loop instantly on level change; `initAudio()` (first user gesture) creates the AudioContext and (re)starts the loop, so music begins right after the child taps Play. Soft sine/triangle notes (vol ~0.08–0.12) sit under the existing sound-effect synth. Verified: node --check, all 11 worlds mapped to defined themes (16/8 seq/bass arrays), Node stubbed-WebAudio harness (melody+bass note counts, per-theme register/scale/rest behavior, seamless theme swap, no-timer-before-gesture).

  - **Music slow-down + per-world ambient + on/off button (2026-08-03, user feedback "too quick, needs more specifics/ambient per level, fine if some levels are slower"):** rebuilt the music engine. (1) Tempos slowed across the board to 58–100bpm (Cave slowest at 58, dark sparse; Egypt/Japan/Autumn 72–78; Spring the bounciest at 100). (2) Melodies lengthened to 32-step loops and bass to 16-beat patterns with per-world movement (Autumn Am–F–G–F, Water/Savanna fifth shifts, Egypt E–B drone), so each loop feels less repetitive and more distinct. (3) NEW ambient sound layer per world, synthesized in Web Audio: Spring birdsong, Summer crickets, Autumn leaf rustle, Winter snow wind (filtered noise), Underground low rumble, Cave echoing drips, Water rising bubbles, Savanna dove coos, Japan shakuhachi-style flute with vibrato, North Pole distant sleigh bells, Egypt desert wind + low hum. Each ambient fires at its own rate/volume underneath the melody. (4) NEW music on/off button (🔊/🔇, next to the 🌍 worlds button) that stops/starts the loop instantly and persists the choice in localStorage (`pkMusic`) so it survives reloads. Scheduler upgraded to lookahead-based (`musicTick` schedules every step falling inside a 0.35s horizon from a 120ms interval) so slow tempos keep rock-steady timing; `musicStepTime`/`musicStep` reset on every theme swap for a clean bar start. Verified: node --check + expanded Node stubbed-WebAudio harness (30 checks: 32/16 array shapes, per-theme registers, rests, slow tempos ≤100bpm, ambient bird 3 chirps per step + wind noise buffers fire, toggle off stops scheduling + on restarts, persisted state, no-timer-before-gesture) — all pass.

### 53. DONE — Add Писање (Tracing) game (2026-08-03)

New standalone page `game/pages/tracing.html` + `game/games/tracing.js` — connect-the-dots tracing with forgiving radius. Hub with 3 activities entered first on open: Слова (all 30 Serbian Cyrillic letters, each as numbered-dot stroke paths, reuse classroom letter names + word/emoji), Бројеви (0–10), Облици (Круг/Квадрат/Троугао/Звезда + 3D wireframes Лопта/Коцка/Квадар/Ваљак/Купа/Пирамида). Auto-playing intro on the hub (pencil draws a squiggle via CSS offset-path + stroke-dash, disabled under prefers-reduced-motion). Interaction: child drags from the pulsing start dot to the next dot; each segment auto-fills within a forgiving radius (15/100 viewBox units); tapping/landing near a later dot auto-completes the skipped segments — impossible to fail. ➡ next button (top-right) jumps to the next letter/number/shape anytime; completion fires the shared 🎉/✏️ celebrate + speaks the name (+ word for letters) ~1.6 s after "Браво!". Progress line "N од 30/11/10" under the caption. All child-facing text Serbian Cyrillic, speech via existing MP3 assets (no new audio). Wired: `game/shared/navigation.js` (game-tracing → pages/tracing.html), `game/shared/main.js` (standalone boot 'tracing'), hub ✏️ button added to `game/index.html` (fills the deferred task-47 orphaned-row finding). Validated: node --check on all modified JS; headless Chrome smoke harness passes 10/10 (hub renders, letters/numbers/shapes load, tracing a full А completes all 3 strokes → "Аутомобил 🚗", next advances Б, back→hub, tap-last-dot auto-fill on number 2, shapes wrap-around, hub button present). 5 screenshots captured to `resources/tracing/*.png` — visual review pending with MiMo V2.5 Free.

  - **Free-draw redesign (2026-08-03, user feedback #2 "dots are not intuitive, kids should just draw on a dashed guide"):** replaced connect-the-dots with FREE DRAW on a dashed guide. The guide is a faint dashed outline of the target (letters/numbers via `GUIDE_FONT='900 288px Fredoka…'` on a `#tracingGuide` canvas, shapes via `drawShapeOutline`), refreshed every `loadItem`, never counted as ink. A compact ref card sits above the canvas. Matching is now metrics-based on a 48×48 ink grid vs the guide's reference grid: pass = `coverage >= MIN_COVER(0.45)` (ink within 4 cells of the guide covers the guide) && `near >= MIN_NEAR(0.8)` (≥80% of ink is within 2 cells of the guide — the decisive anti-blob/anti-scribble rule) && `ink <= MAX_INK(2000)` (rejects dense scribbles). `MIN_INK=40` drops accidental single taps. Measured separation (tools/tracing_probe.js): genuine traces near 0.98–1.0, blobs 0.69, scribbles 0.62, wrong single line 0.7 — huge margin. Correct drawing → celebrate (name + word spoken) then auto-advance after 2600 ms (`glyphDone` → `loadItem(index+1)`, which clears ink + redraws guide). Wrong → "Покушај још једном!" nudge, canvas stays so the child can draw over. New layout: `#tracingBoard` (aspect-ratio 1/1, min(50vmin,34vh)) wraps canvas + guide (both absolute inset:0, 400×400), ref above, caption below, activity column `overflow-y:auto` — no overlap at 390×844/844×390/768×1024. Shape activity now lists ONLY the 4 flat shapes (Круг/Квадрат/Троугао/Звезда); 3D wireframes dropped per user decision. `window.__traceDebug` (`matchResult`/`refGrid`/`inkGrid`) kept for the probe. Validated: node --check; canonical smoke `node tools/tracing_smoke.js` — 22/22 PASS (hub, dashed guide present, layout no-overlap, empty-canvas nudge, pointer paint, clear keeps guide, correct А/0/Круг accepted, sloppy Б accepted, auto-advance А→Б→В / 0→1 / Круг→Квадрат, line/scribble/blob rejected, shape wrap, hub button).

  - **Hub guiding illustration (2026-08-03, user request: "above the 3 buttons make a reference letter and a pencil over a canvas where the letter is partially drawn — acts as a visual instruction"):** replaced the plain squiggle intro with a static-style SVG demo in `#introDemo` (viewBox 0 0 200 120): a lavender reference card with solid А (matches `#tracingRef` styling) + a white canvas showing the SAME letter as a dashed guide (`#introGuide`, stroke-opacity .35, dasharray 4 5) with a solid partially-drawn ink path (`#introInk`) and the ✏️ pencil (`#introPencil`) parked at the draw tip. Letter geometry is a proper upright А: apex (137,30) → bottom-left (121,98) → bottom-right (153,98), crossbar 128–146 at y=64. Per user feedback the crossbar stays undrawn (guide-only) and the pencil sits ON it: ink path = the two legs only, `stroke-dasharray:105 2000` (105 = left leg 69.9 + 50% of the right leg 35.1, so the ink tip lands exactly at the crossbar's right end 145,64 where the pencil is). Looping animation: ink draws in over 30% (dashoffset 105→0), holds the partial state to 70%, wipes back; pencil gently bobs; all disabled under `prefers-reduced-motion` (static partial state). Smoke hub check strengthened: verifies refText А, guide dasharray `4 5`, ink dasharray `105 2000`, pencil present. node --check passes; `node tools/tracing_smoke.js` 22/22 PASS.

### 54. DONE — Dev tooling: consolidated headless harnesses into `tools/` (2026-08-03)

Per user request for a reusable tooling home. New `tools/headless.js` shared harness: serves `game/` over HTTP, boots headless Chrome on a UNIQUE temp profile per run (`pkv-{tag}-{ts}-{rand}`, debug port derived from the http port + offset + rand) with retries and MIME map incl. woff2/png/ogg/mp3, returns `{evalv, navigate, close, port}` and exports `check(name, ok, info)`, `sleep`, `getFails()`; `close()` kills only that run's Chrome (by profile tag in the process command line) so parallel runs and crashes can't hold stale profile locks. Migrated the canonical tracing smoke from temp to `tools/tracing_smoke.js` (22 checks, requires `./headless.js`), added `tools/tracing_probe.js` (matching-metric table for threshold tuning, uses `window.__traceDebug`) and `tools/dilate_test.js` (integral-image dilate correctness). `tools/README.md` documents files, commands (`node tools/dilate_test.js`, `node tools/tracing_probe.js`, `node tools/tracing_smoke.js`), HTTP-not-file:// rule, stale-Chrome kill command, and the match rule + measured thresholds. AGENTS.md working-rhythm updated (tools/ + tracing_smoke command; tools/ and resources/ are dev-only, game/ stays deployable-only). Validated: all three tools run green from the repo root.

Four Phase 4 adventure games planned as separate tasks, each fully playable and session-sized. Reuse kitty's music/ambient synth, HUD, worlds picker, particles, decor. BUILD (2026-08-04): engine written (`game/games/adventure.js` ~1470 lines — port of the kitty runtime into a config-driven engine; modes `ground`/`drive`/`fly`; shared HUD ids `#adv-*`; worlds picker, win modal, 🔊/🌍 buttons, particles, decor, procedural music: 32-step melody + 16-beat bass + 13 ambient layers (bird/cricket/wind/rustle/rumble/drip/bubble/flute/bell/coo/desert/horn/waves); debug handle `window.__adv` + `window.AdventureEngine.create` for headless tests, incl. `lastHitAt`/`bumpCount`). Driving game (`game/games/driving.js`) = mode `drive`, hero 🚗, 10 road worlds (Градски трг, Поље сунцокрета, Јесења шума, Зимски пут, Планински пут, Ноћни град, Пустињска магистрала, Тропско острво, Морска обала, Космичка стаза), each with own obstacles (cars/trucks/buses/cones/rocks/barriers), 13 coins, goal gate, decor (city/palms/gulls/stars/leaves/snow), night + cosmic themes, and a unique music theme per world. Page `game/pages/driving.html` (title + back btn + shared adventure.css). Wired: hub 🚗 button (`data-go="game-driving"`), `shared/navigation.js` route, `shared/main.js` standalone boot. Engine bug found+fixed during smoke: drive-mode `respawnPlayer()` knocked `cameraX` back 160 but left `player.x` stale until the next frame — visible one-frame teleport + wrong goal/coin hitbox; now `player.x = cameraX + player.offsetX` immediately on respawn. Canonical validation `node tools/driving_smoke.js` 17/17 PASS (boot, 10 worlds config, 10 music themes 32/16, steering, clamps, coin pickup, obstacle knockback, goal→win modal, next level, worlds picker 10 worlds + jump, music toggle, hub/navigation/standalone wiring). ### 62. DONE — Дино (Dino) game (2026-08-05)

Third Phase 4 adventure game on the shared engine from task 60. Ground mode (kitty physics: jump/gravity/platforms): hero 🦕 (fontSize 78, heroW 110, heroH 100), 10 prehistoric worlds (Прашума, Тропска долина, Језеро, Вулкан, Пећина, Мочвара, Ледено доба, Пустиња, Ноћни свет, Острво диносауруса), solid grounds + floating platforms + moving platforms (`moves` minX/maxX/vx) + auto step-up stairs + pipes that pop out a mini-raptor every 3s, ~15 coins + ЦИЉ goal gate each, 10 unique music themes (32-step melody + 16-beat bass + root + ambient per world), 10 decor types (jungle/valley/lake/volcano/cave/swamp/ice/desert/night/island — trees, flowers, lake lilies, erupting volcanoes, crystal stalactites, reeds, snow, cacti, stars, palms), Пећина has a rocky ceiling, Ноћни свет is a night world, goal = stone temple arch + „ЦИЉ" banner. Game hooks: `cfg.drawEnemy`, `cfg.drawDecor`, `cfg.drawGoal`, `cfg.heroFlip: true` (🦕 faces left natively). Page `game/pages/dino.html` with tablet ground controls (`#adv-controls.adv-ground-controls` — ◀▶ cluster left, big ⬆ jump button right, `data-adv="jump"`; new CSS in adventure.css). ### 71b. DONE — Adventure bottom-cutoff still on-device after the 71 dvh fix — FIXED (2026-08-06, Ponytail Lazy Dev)

User: on the tablet the bottom is still cut, e.g. the left buttons show only the top half. Root cause: on a browser WITHOUT `dvh` support (older Android WebView / Samsung Internet) `height:100dvh` is ignored and the page falls back to `100vh`, which extends under the address bar → bottom controls clipped. A CSS-only `100svh` fallback can't help those browsers (svh ships in the same engine generation as dvh), so the fix is JS: `game/games/adventure.js` (the shared engine loaded by all 4 adventure pages) now pins `#adv-game` height to `window.innerHeight` — the universally-correct visible-viewport height on every mobile browser — and re-syncs it on `resize`, `orientationchange`, and `visibilitychange` (address-bar show/hide + rotation; the visibility handler defers ~100ms). Inline style overrides the CSS; on desktop `innerHeight` == layout viewport, so nothing changes there. The CSS `height:100vh; height:100dvh;` stays as the pre-JS baseline. Validated: `node --check` clean; **driving 17/17, ocean 20/20, dino 40/40, space 21/21 PASS** (one driving coin-pickup FAIL on the first run was a pre-existing test-order flake — the steering check had already collected `coins[0]` — clean on re-run; unrelated to this change, which only sets the container height to the emulated viewport). docs/ re-synced. **User on-device tablet re-test CONFIRMED done (2026-08-07, per user) — no further cutoff reported.**

### 72b. DONE — "Dinos not visible at all" bug — FIXED (2026-08-06, Ponytail Lazy Dev)

Root cause (proven by headless probes): `loadDinoFrame`'s pixel crop called `getImageData` on a canvas drawn from the PNG — under `file://` the canvas is tainted and `getImageData` throws SecurityError, so `onload` aborted before `dinoFrames[type]` was set → invisible dino. Over HTTP (the supported Live Server path) it rendered fine (A/B pixel diff of the player region = 4596 changed px). Fix: `getImageData` wrapped in try/catch with a hardcoded `DINO_CROP_FALLBACK` (exact runtime-measured crop rects: bronto `{x:264,y:106,w:1529,h:1633}`, t_rex `{x:154,y:112,w:1691,h:1775}`) — drawing still works under file://, only the readback is blocked. Re-verified: file:// probe now loads both frames (`b:true,t:true`); HTTP probe still renders. Added a dino-smoke regression for the fallback constants; **dino smoke 41/41 PASS**, exit 0. docs/ re-synced. Note: the supported way to play is still Live Server over HTTP (file:// also breaks audio + the kitty iframe); the fallback just stops the dino from being invisible.

### 73. DONE — Папир Маца → Мала истраживачица: full character rebuild (2026-08-06, Ponytail Lazy Dev)

Implemented + validated — see plan below. **Verification:** extracted papper_kitty inline script `node --check` clean; **kitty smoke 29/29 PASS** (all task73 checks: rename, 6 frames 35×48 feet-flush, state machine idle→walk→run→jump, accel ramp `v1=0.55`, idle cycling, facing mirror; all pre-existing checks too). Two smoke bugs fixed along the way (both test-only, game was correct): (1) the state-machine check raced a random-shuffled world's walker/mice/pipes → test now zeroes `walkers`/`mice` and anchors the player at x=40 on ground 0; (2) the pre-existing `resize` flake was the check requiring the player to land on `platforms[0]` only — in some worlds the spawn sits on a `dy:80` float (feet y=932 = 980−48), which is on solid ground but not the ground platform; settle check now accepts "top of any platform". docs/ re-synced. **Follow-up 2026-08-06 (per user play-test feedback "no bobbing, make it larger"):** the grounded bob (`sin(bobPhase)*2.5`) is REMOVED (`bobPhase` field + update increment + draw offset deleted; smoke static check now asserts NO `bobPhase`) and the hero sprite is ENLARGED from 35×48 → **43×60** (`EXPLORER_SPRITE_H 48→60`, draw anchored feet-flush via `player.height/2 - EXPLORER_SPRITE_H` so only the head overhangs the 48×48 hitbox ~12px upward; hitbox unchanged — levels stay tuned to it). **Root-caused the long-standing kitty-smoke flakiness too (all test-only, game was correct):** (a) the `mirror` check's color-SUM signature is position-independent — a fully-enclosed hero mirrors colors to mirrored positions, so the sum is unchanged (`differ:false`); the old region only passed because it captured a *partial* hero. Fixed with a position-aware pixel-diff (`> 200` changed bytes in the region). (b) the `stomp` check dropped the player at `walkers[0].x - 10`, which is OUTSIDE the patrol `[x, x+range]` — the stomp only fired if the walker happened to be near its minX when the feet crossed the hit band (random world shuffle) → now freezes `walkers[0].vx=0` and drops the player straight onto the walker (deterministic). (c) the `side` check placed the player 30px left of the walker — a possible pit (walkers sit at their ground's left edge) → now places the player exactly on the walker, which triggers the side-hit branch deterministically (feet at `groundY` fail the stomp's `<= groundY-6`). Validated: extracted script `node --check` clean; **kitty smoke 29/29 PASS × 3 consecutive runs**. docs/ re-synced. Awaiting user commit/push + on-device visual check. **Follow-up 2026-08-06 (per user play-test feedback "running is frozen in the run sprite" + "hub button icon"):** (a) **running now alternates between the run (03) and walk (02) frames** (`currentExplorerFrame()` run branch: `floor(animTime/10)%2` → ~167ms per stride instead of a frozen run pose); state-machine smoke's run assertion relaxed to "run state uses run-or-walk frame" (frame-selection coverage moved to a new dedicated check), new check "running alternates between the run and walk frames". (b) **hub game button icon is now the character itself** — `game/index.html` kitty button `🧭` → `<img src="assets/images/explorer/03_run_right.png" alt="Мала истраживачица">` + `.hub-btn img{width:78%;height:78%;object-fit:contain}`. (4) rename the game to **Мала истраживачица** (hub icon 🐱→🧭, iframe title, page title, favicon), and replace the cat-meow hit sound with a soft synth "thud" (a girl explorer shouldn't meow). **Sprite facts (measured 2026-08-06, System.Drawing pixel scan of all 14 PNGs):** every frame is 237×352 with the character horizontally centered and **feet bottom-aligned at opaque row 328 (bottomGap = 23 transparent rows, identical in all frames)** — verified, so one draw offset works for every frame. Opaque art ≈ 185–193 wide × 277–307 tall. Native right-facing = idle×3 (01/04/06), walk (02), run (03), jump (05); left set (10–14) + profile (07/09) are exact mirrors, back view (08). Only frames 01–06 get deployed (left = runtime mirror, the engine already flips with `ctx.scale(±1, squish)`). **Implementation plan:** (a) copy 01–06 to `game/assets/images/explorer/`; (b) pre-render each to an offscreen canvas scaled from source rect `(0,0,237,329)` (full canvas minus the uniform 23-row bottom gap) → 35×48, feet exactly at the frame bottom — **no `getImageData`, no pixel crop, no `file://` taint problem** (unlike the dino sprites), draw at `(-w/2, -24)` from the hitbox-centre origin; (c) state machine resolved each `update()`: airborne → `jump` (05), else |vx|<1.2 → `idle` (cycle 01→04→06, `floor(animTime/22)%3` ≈ 366ms/frame), |vx|<2.6 → `walk` (02), else `run` (03); `player.animTime += 1` per fixed 60fps step (deterministic for headless smokes); (d) acceleration `vx ±= 0.55` toward ±`speed` (5.2), friction `×0.8` when no input; (e) keep the grounded bob (`sin(bobPhase)*2.5`, faster while running) + squish/stretch juice, add a landing squash (`squish = 0.8` on airborne→grounded); (f) delete `drawCostume` + its call + the 11 `costume:` world keys; swap `playCatSound`/`kittyCatAudio` (cat.ogg) for a synth `playHurtSound` (sine 180→70Hz, ~0.22s); (g) rename title/favicon/iframe. Smoke: extend `tools/kitty_smoke.js` with 6 frames loaded (pixel readback over HTTP), state selection idle/walk/run/jump, feet-flush row check, facing mirror, idle frame cycling, acceleration ramp — all existing checks stay green. Then docs/ re-sync + README/HANDOVER.

### 167. DONE — Single source of truth for the app list (2026-10-01, Ponytail Lazy Dev)

Roadmap R7. `game/data/app-registry.js` is now the only place the app/page/route list exists, and seven tools that each carried their own copy were rewired onto one shared loader (`tools/registry.js`: `all`, `children`, `parents`, `byId`, `byRoute`, `roleOf`, `routesByGroup`, `allHubRoutes`).

Runtime:
- `game/shared/navigation.js` and `game/shared/main.js` resolve routes and boot state from the registry instead of hardcoded tables; the 14 pages that use shared main boot load `data/app-registry.js` before `shared/main.js` (self-wiring `animal_counting` / `animal_memory` / `animal_puzzle` intentionally unchanged, and the registry records that with `back: null, start: null`).
- Registry fields are limited to what a live consumer reads — `route`, `hubGroup`, `hubOrder`, `back`, `start` — plus `role` derived from `category`. No speculative fields.

Tools rewired: `offline_smoke.mjs` (16 children, parent URL and 17 hub buttons all derived — its hardcoded parent count drifted before), `hub_smoke.js`, `run_all.js`, `axe_check.js`, `play_matrix.mjs`, `touch_interruption_core.js` (all 17 entries including `parent` preserved), `screenshot.js`, `validate_pages.js`.

Validation:
- `validate_pages.js` now enforces the contract (unique id/route, path exists under `pages/`, page present in the registry — **promoted from warning to failure**, hub button exists for every route, retired route absent from both registry and visible hub markup, unique hubOrder per group, valid `back` id, defined `start` global).
- Three guard negative tests, because **a check that cannot fail proves nothing**: `guards/registry_guards_negtest.js` (12 mutations, each must produce the guard's own message — a bare non-zero exit lets an unrelated failure be credited), `guards/route_contract_negtest.js` (6 wrong-wiring forms + real-wiring control; `route_contract.js` replaced ten smokes' hand-written greps for route tables that no longer exist, which passed vacuously), `guards/games_map_negtest.js` (the derived game-file→smoke mapping). Wired into `check_release.js` and the release CI job, **not** `check_fast` — they edit and restore a tracked source file, which a read-only gate must never do.

Deleted: `tools/racing_probe.js` and its two stale PNGs — it probed `pages/racing.html`, deleted in task 156.

### 168. DONE — Three harness bugs that made checks pass while proving nothing (2026-10-01, Ponytail Lazy Dev)

Found while closing out R7. All three are the same family as the task-120 anti-pattern.

1. **Chrome debug port derived arithmetically** (`headless.js`, was `httpPort + 100 + Math.random()*1000`). Windows hands out ephemeral ports around 49582-57766 and Hyper-V/WinNAT reserves 17 blocks inside that span, so ~9% of derived ports were unbindable. Chrome could not listen, never published `/json/version`, and the smoke exited non-zero with **0 checks** — the intermittent `touch_interruption_b` failure that had been attributed to host load for several tasks. Now `freePort()` (`listen(0)`). Negative-tested: reverting only that line reproduces the failure on a port inside an excluded range.
2. **`close()` was not awaited.** It fired `killChromeByTag` and returned, so a tool booting several browsers in one process raced a still-dying Chrome. It is now `async`, waits for the port to go quiet, and deletes the profile dir — 1503 stale profiles had accumulated in `%TEMP%` (**32 GB**, now cleared).
3. **`screenshot.js` could not fail.** It called `navigate('/index.html')` with a relative URL, which never resolves from `about:blank` (every capture was a blank 4.7 KB PNG); it applied `Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride` *before* navigating, and the override is dropped on a cross-document navigation; and it omitted `deviceScaleFactor`, without which Chrome silently ignores the entire call — so phone/tablet/desktop produced three byte-identical files. It now navigates absolutely, waits for readiness, sets the override after load, and **fails** if any capture is under 20 KB or if N captures have fewer than N distinct sizes. Both regressions negative-tested.

Also chased and disproved: piped stdio as a boot blocker (Chrome writes ~100 bytes; boots pass with `stdio: 'ignore'` too) and port overflow past 65535 (ephemeral ports never get that high here). Boot patience is 4 attempts / ~24 s with a message stating "no assertions ran".

**Validated:** `run_all.js --concurrency 2` — **ALL 24 TOOLS PASS, 492 checks**. `check_fast.js` 6/6 green (writes nothing). `check_release.js` green, nothing written. `offline_smoke.mjs` 16/16. All three guard suites green. `validate_pages.js` 17 pages OK. `git diff --check` clean.

### 169. DONE — Runtime Error / Console / Unhandled-Rejection Gate (2026-10-01, Ponytail Lazy Dev)

Roadmap R8. `tools/headless.js` now collects page exceptions, `console.error`, unhandled rejections, and failures for same-origin runtime resources, and checks the collection before each headless smoke exits. Service-worker registration warnings are captured narrowly. `tools/runtime_error_smoke.js` scans all registry pages plus the hub and negative-tests each capture path by provoking the corresponding failure; the generated CI smoke matrix includes it automatically. `play_matrix.mjs` adds unhandled-rejection and local-resource collection.

This gate found a production exception in `game/games/animal_puzzle.js`: the space scene passed six arguments to `CanvasRenderingContext2D.ellipse()`; fixed by supplying the missing rotation argument. The shapes smoke's synthetic pointer events were also calling the native pointer-capture API without an active browser pointer; the fixture now isolates that API precondition rather than allowing a synthetic test error to mask product faults. The service-worker subpath smoke now starts without navigating to a nonexistent path on its unused local server.

Validation: **25/25 tools, 554 checks, 0 failures** (`node tools/run_all.js --concurrency 2`); fast gate 6/6; offline E2E all 16 games; Chromium hub matrix 5/5 viewports; generated artifact validation 4/4. `docs/` synced to `game/` and the canonical offline manifest regenerated. The Windows ZIP command remains unavailable on this host; no ZIP was added to `docs/`, preserving the required exact mirror and the settled hidden-link decision.

## Appendix: Task 11 expected behavior

Animal Scene Puzzle must follow this interaction:

- Begin each round with a clickable animal scene in a natural setting, such as a playground, house, or savanna.
- When the scene is tapped, hide it and show a same-sized centered placeholder.
- Split the scene into puzzle pieces and scatter the pieces outside the placeholder.
- Let the child drag pieces into their correct positions with forgiving placement.
- Play a satisfying pop sound whenever a piece is placed correctly.
- When every piece is placed, play the celebration sound, show a celebration icon, and reveal a button for the next puzzle.
- Keep all scenes and assets offline and toddler-friendly.
